- **style-calibrator**: Use this agent when /sws:calibrate-style is invoked. Builds a reusable, evidence-backed voice profile from the author's own papers in Zotero via an iterative held-out matching loop. Runs the 5 phases: discover (name-search the Zotero library, rank hits by authorship position), flag&split (author curates; auto-hold-out 1-2 recent first-author papers), extract (read training PDFs, pool by section), 3.5 evolution (diachronic style trajectory), calibrate-loop (per section type: draft candidate -&gt; generate held-out prose on its real content -&gt; score via sws_stylometry.py + a Haiku voice-similarity… ([piripocchio8/scientific-writing-superpowers](https://github.com/piripocchio8/scientific-writing-superpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/style-calibrator.md))

Captures and maintains the repository's unique "coding personality". ALWAYS run all AgentCheck agents in parallel using multiple Task calls in single message. - **supervisor**: Controls the round loop and owns the one judgement a script cannot make - which LAYER a stop point lives in. ([ekamphuis82/claude-code-swarm](https://github.com/ekamphuis82/claude-code-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-tester.md))
- **swarm-triage**: Summarizes a Rensei child session's transcript/receipt into a short, structured status report. ([RenseiAI/claude-plugin](https://github.com/RenseiAI/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/rensei/agents/swarm-triage.md))
- **swarm-validator**: Post-merge validator. Verifies merged work actually helped — runs relevant checks after each merge type. Catches regressions. Creates priority issues for failures. - **swarm-worker**: Execute a single swarm task in an isolated git worktree. Receive a fully-specified recipe from the main context — a scenario recipe plus an implementation contract — then run `Skill(scenario)` followed by `Skill(implement)` and report JSON status. Make no design decisions and do not expand scope. Invoked exclusively by `/swarm-dispatch`; never elsewhere. ([friedbotstudio/baseline](https://github.com/friedbotstudio/baseline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/swarm-worker.md) · ★14)
- **swarm-worker**: Autonomous task executor for devloop:run-swarm. Implements a single plan task with fresh context. Do not use for interactive work. Use when: Orchestrator is running swarm mode and needs to parallelize independent plan tasks. Do NOT use when: Interactive work is needed, or for single-task execution (just do the work directly). &lt;example&gt; Context: The orchestrator is running devloop:run-swarm and needs to execute a plan task assistant: "I'll spawn a swarm-worker to implement this task with fresh context." &lt;/example&gt; ([Zate/cc-plugins](https://github.com/Zate/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/devloop/agents/swarm-worker.md) · ★10)
- **swarm-worker**: one swarm worker for a slice or race arm. ([edheltzel/Do-Pstack](https://github.com/edheltzel/Do-Pstack/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-worker.md) · ★2)
- **swarm-worker-contracts**: Defines the strict JSON schemas for all Swarm dispatch and result payloads. Every `WorkerRequest` and `WorkerResult` MUST conform to these schemas. Used by: `supervisor-agent`, `swarm_dispatcher.js`, `/swarm` workflow. ([Harmitx7/tribunal-kit](https://github.com/Harmitx7/tribunal-kit/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/swarm-worker-contracts.md) · ★5)
- **swarm-worker-haiku**: Use this agent for simple swarm tasks: deletions, renames, config changes, boilerplate generation. &lt;example&gt; Context: Swarm orchestrator assigns task "Delete old file src/old-code.ts" user: "Execute task 3.1: Delete file" assistant: "[Spawns swarm-worker-haiku to handle deletion]" &lt;commentary&gt; Simple task, no complex logic - haiku is sufficient. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([amoscicki/aromatt](https://github.com/amoscicki/aromatt/blob/HEAD/swarm/agents/swarm-worker-haiku.md) · 2 copies)
- **swarm-worker-kit**: **Purpose:** Execute assigned implementation tasks as part of a parallel swarm. Implementation only — no quality checks, no commits, no PRs. ([hamiltonroad/checked-out](https://github.com/hamiltonroad/checked-out/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/swarm-worker-kit.md))
- **swarm-worker-opus**: Use this agent for standard swarm tasks: feature implementation, integration, bug fixes, service creation. &lt;example&gt; Context: Swarm orchestrator assigns task "Implement booking validation service" user: "Execute task 2.3: Create validation service" assistant: "[Spawns swarm-worker-opus for implementation]" &lt;commentary&gt; Standard implementation task requiring code understanding - opus handles this. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([amoscicki/aromatt](https://github.com/amoscicki/aromatt/blob/HEAD/swarm/agents/swarm-worker-opus.md) · 2 copies)

- **swarm-worker-ultrathink**: Use this agent for complex swarm tasks: architecture decisions, algorithms, edge cases, schema interpreters. &lt;example&gt; Context: Swarm orchestrator assigns task "Implement JSON Schema to Effect Schema interpreter" user: "Execute task 3.3: Create restoreSchema function" assistant: "[Spawns swarm-worker-ultrathink for complex algorithm]" &lt;commentary&gt; Complex interpreter with recursive logic and edge cases - ultrathink required. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([amoscicki/aromatt](https://github.com/amoscicki/aromatt/blob/HEAD/swarm/agents/swarm-worker-ultrathink.md) · 2 copies)

- **sweep-auditor**: Completeness adversary - finds the member of a should-be-uniform set that diverged (the incomplete sweep: a change applied to N-1 of N sites). Orthogonal to the house happy/sad-path signature AND to the seam/property/interleaving trio - their nulls are joins, input spaces, and schedules; this null is the relation across a SET of peers. Dispatch after a migration / rename / new-variant / dual-surface change, during audits, or from the idle loop. Writes a completeness guard; the deliverable is a straggler (a bug) or a durable exhaustiveness test. (#1826 family - the fourth orthogonal shape.)
